# Development plan: IQToolkit modernization

**Guide version:** Development Plan Creation Guide 2.7.1, 2026-10-02; Autonomous Development Plan Guide, inspected 2026-10-02.
**Mode:** Vertical-Slice.
**Plan type:** Public Library / Existing-System Feature.
**Planning horizon:** Rolling-Wave. V0, ready M0 and MDB0 archive-acquisition preparation work are expanded below; later work retains its complete scope and gates and must be expanded before dispatch.
**Execution strategy:** Bounded Parallel for independent files; sequential integration and phase closure.
**Workspace:** Base branch `master`; execution branch `agent/modernization-roadmap`, created at the same revision without discarding changes. The initial index was empty. The only pre-existing work was the untracked roadmap; all subsequent plan/tooling/metadata changes are task-owned. Shared uncommitted files require the explicit packet ownership below.
**Execution baseline:** Initial baseline `60ddef6`; latest code/build verification baseline `e9c9ab83efaddb76eb761bbd1468f16b83623867` on `agent/modernization-roadmap`. See [resumption handoff](modernization-handoff.md) for the documentation handoff and current next-step gates. Roadmap draft 0.2 is retained as a historical predecessor; [Roadmap 0.3](modernization-roadmap.md) is the governing current requirements input.
**Current requirements revision:** Roadmap0.3, refined through the requested Oracle review. Shared features now use the M1q selection/deferral track; M6 explicitly includes Valkey and depends on M1. Oracle CLEAR covers document clarity only.
**Baseline commands:** `dotnet build IQToolkit.sln -c Release -warnaserror`; `dotnet test IQToolkit.sln -c Release --no-build`; `dotnet run --project Test -c Release --no-build`.
**Observed baseline:** Zero build warnings/errors; 7 MSTest tests pass, 325 SQL Server integration tests skip because their environments are absent; 247 translation checks pass. These results do not certify SQL Server, MySQL, Access, or new providers.
**Coordination:** Root integrates; Oracle reviews integrated work. At most three workers plus the coordinator/reviewer within the four-agent harness. Workers cannot delegate further without a revised packet. Separate write scopes share this checkout; build/test operations are serialized by root unless explicitly handed off. No concurrent use of the same test database.
**Dispatch budget:** Three evidence-driven repair attempts per failure; stop affected work and record the unmet prerequisite after that. No user token or cost budget was supplied. No paid service calls or deployments are authorized by this plan.
**Ancillary tool standard:** New MP0 and VP0 ignored stdlib command tools use the adopted Python Code Standards revision 2026-09-29, SHA256 `05269f75a22ab7a66712bdfb0aeb628f3b4f22e57fe2981f815f9f72f89445b5`, under their local single-host scopes. MDB0.1a/1b use that same adopted revision as governing for this scoped controller tooling; the live 2026-10-02 source (SHA256 `c613491cc698578341dd788f7110fbd1a8b15097fd8eb0b84095f3a2f60e8498`) is also read, compared, and bound in current review inputs. The delta updates version-status facts (3.14.8; 3.15 RC3/final timing), adds measured free-threaded 3.14 observations, and refreshes CI action majors. Those changes do not apply to the pinned Python3.12.3 single-host controls: this scope adds no Python application/runtime support, free-threaded execution, CI workflow, or action-pin change. Both revisions retain the Python3.12+ floor and existing 3.12 projects need no unrelated runtime migration. No replacement adoption is made; reassess the live standard before expanding Python/runtime/CI scope.
**Action authority:** The owner resumed the overall roadmap implementation on 2026-10-10. Local investigation, implementation, and verification may proceed within the plan's task-specific scope and gates. The owner later explicitly authorized committing and pushing these phase changes once the checkpoint is stable and reviewable, then resuming. Apply that conditional authorization only after exact-candidate planning acceptance and the MDB0.1a expected controls are verified, with a reviewed task-scoped diff. It authorizes no release, tag, publication or operational external action. It does not authorize package/network acquisition, SDK/database execution, source rebinding, or provider promotion beyond the explicit per-task gates below. Preserve unrelated work.
**Standards:** Technical Markdown Style Guide applies to documents; `agent-tooling.md` applies to tool routing. The owner explicitly selected `/home/firestrand/Downloads/Cross-Platform-DotNet-Engineering-Standard-1.1.0-draft.1.md` on 2026-10-02: `ENG-STD-DOTNET-XPLAT`, version 1.1.0-draft.1, SHA-256 `8c56930ec1cd26e8b58a273d47cb0b9713f55e6d4961b6d29a636ffaf4f0ebe2`. Its technical rules govern C#/.NET work; the development/autonomous guides govern execution and data authorization. Draft status does not require reasking after this explicit selection. Apply DOTNET-GOV-005's legacy adoption scope, and do not claim whole-project compliance from scoped changes.
**Real data policy:** Record provenance for every new evidence input; inherited synthetic test rows are not automatically approved. Real engine execution and representative datasets are required for certification and quality claims.
**Generated data authorization:** The project owner authorized deterministic internal controller-state fixtures for MDB0.1 only on 2026-10-06; the exact purpose, handwritten generator, deterministic values, and covered test task are recorded under Task V0.2. No provider payloads, package contents, domain records, query fixtures, or representative workload data are authorized. The separate C# fixture decision in V0.2 remains pending; no other task may consume generated or synthetic data without a new scope-specific decision.
**Provider policy:** Retain SQL translation/execution seams; isolate SDKs in optional adapters. Shared contracts describe capabilities without claiming interchangeable database semantics.
**Readiness:** SDK 10.0.401/runtime 10.0.12 and SQLite are available on Linux ARM64. Docker's executable exists, but its socket is inaccessible; no remote database or AWS certification environment has been verified. Graphify and Context7 are unavailable. Serena works in the investigation worker; its main-session language server failed initialization, so that session uses `rg` fallback.
**Ordering:** Verify the existing system and repair release-critical cache/resource defects before async execution. Add true driver async execution while preserving sync behavior, then SQL capability contracts and PostgreSQL, explicit keyed DynamoDB, separately accepted vector/search slices, and analytics. Data/contract gates precede each dependent adapter; deferred candidate selection remains a real decision, not a completed feature.
**Fact policy:** Requirement-owned public behavior is Tier 1; narrow assertions bind to named evidence and declared fixtures. A passing legacy suite protects observed cases, not every promised future behavior.

**Historical tooling checkpoint (draft 0.107):** The reusable nullable-column continuity class and its standalone coordinator operation are verified in scope; private Release compilation has zero warnings/errors, the accepted descriptor reconstructs the exact qualified comparison and an authentic older descriptor is rejected. Source, packet and observed Oracle reviews are CLEAR. [ATLAS and evidence](modernization-nullable-continuity-hardness.md) record all 1,376 preparation seals, 829/7 control captures, unchanged 336 ordinary binaries and complete private retirement. Wrapper edits only supply the new class to its existing private compilation. Older anchor replay, active descriptor selection, registration, cache compatibility and current-final acceptance remain open; the unchanged profile retains draft 0.106 documentation pins. All 34 ledger rows and historical 33-fact/87-method bindings remain unchanged. This is progress toward V1ai.4, not its closure or full M0/roadmap completion.

**Historical MariaDB checkpoint (draft 0.124):** This is a retained earlier checkpoint; it is superseded for resumption by [the current handoff](modernization-handoff.md). Its packet and review status must not be treated as current. The historical preparation protected 358 non-ignored paths, the index, and 336 ordinary binaries. It did not establish runtime compatibility or publisher-trust certification.

Task MDB0.1b-T produced the expected six-test normal RED with only the late-stage descriptor-close assertion failing; Task MDB0.1b then added function-wide directory-descriptor cleanup. The corrected normal/-O suites pass all six tests, and static normal/-O checks pass all eight checks. Evidence is recorded in the MDB0.1b ATLAS and five immutable 0400 JSON results; the original LockoutTests class remains byte-identical. The historical earlier GREEN paths remain untouched. Round85 source review returned CLEAR for exact candidate Round4 (27 verified inputs), closing `MDB0.1b-SRC-001` for this source/evidence epoch. The report SHA256 is `67787a099ec58400b7616db24690972a5de75b6756b229096f4cd5778b53b4ab`; details are in the [resumption handoff](modernization-handoff.md) and [MDB0.1b ATLAS](modernization-mariadb-lockout-hardness.md). Roadmap 0.3 is the governing requirements revision; draft 0.2 is retained as historical. Round87 unconditionally accepted plan0.199 for its exact 201-input manifest (report SHA256 `662fcb3044e66898dd5e661d84d6ea7c496ba6b49b7519a5a0b15937fe2923fd`), closing MDB0.2-PLAN-001. Round88 reviewed plan0.200 and returned CHANGES_REQUIRED for MDB0.2-PLAN-002/003; this draft corrects the revision label and uses detached candidate state for changing next-action status. Planning PASS and current source CLEAR remain required before any separately authorized new packet preparation. Do not reuse the stale attempt or dispatch.

**Historical continuity checkpoint (draft 0.109):** V1aj.1 causal RED and V1aj.2a R1 full replay/wrong-context controls are observed Oracle CLEAR. The canonical port reconstructs its authentic 195-input comparison; nullable replay preserves 409-input qualification. Eleven exact hash-bound alias identities cover nine logical paths before complete older replay. The strict private build has zero warnings/errors; native exits 0/0/0/0/1 select descriptor 378b and reject the authentic wrong-context input without a pointer. All 3,310 preparation seals, 3,927 native capture pairs, source/index/336 binaries and five settled sessions verify; 61 files are retained inactive and the original parent is absent. [ATLAS](modernization-recent-final-continuity-hardness.md) records the genuine first failed attempt, exact PDB-context correction and repair 1 of 3.

Registration is prepared for 34 facts / 95 methods, preserving 33 prior raw rows and claims; fresh classification remains unexecuted. Cache compatibility, checked consumer lock/feed refresh and a complete fresh nine-component final chain remain required by V1aj.2–4. The unchanged profile retains historical documentation/cache identities; current-final acceptance, targets, full M0/roadmap and all owner gates remain open. Only plan/state/new ATLAS change after the tested draft 0.108 snapshot; source/native receipts retain that historical identity.

**Historical bounded-stop checkpoint (draft 0.110):** V1aj.2 consumer refresh/cache compatibility and V1aj.3 private controls have observed Oracle CLEAR. Eight checked consumer files match the authentic generated feed: seven complete graphs change only 18 toolkit content hashes, and six archive pins change. Cache replay preserves the original diagnostic seed; full preflight and three authentic rejection controls pass their expected outcomes. These are scoped tooling results.

V1aj.4 fresh run `fresh-final.k2nm113b` / `run.33Yxvef9` passed all nine native producer stages with zero build warnings/errors: 435 definitions, 110 passes, 325 explicit SQL Server skips and 247 translations. Coverage measures 2,968/8,359 lines and 1,602/5,336 branches across 97 sources and 16,736 leaves. Its admission callback failed before assessment because it required `passed` for an authentic `prepared` directory. The first filesystem recovery then failed before SDK dispatch because admission recursively selected unrelated native platform outputs.

The corrected, separately reviewed recovery `assessment-recovery.8xvjjknw` ran SDK identity 0 / assessment 1, but the native result rejects **evidence integrity**, not final readiness: `Native consumer session identity differs.` The authentic witness records PID/PGID/SID 574814 with leading `ps` padding; the digits-only start anchor in `PackageIndependentEvidence.cs:536` rejects that representation. All 5,585 seal/snapshot pairs, 13,780 protected files, source/index and 336 ordinary binaries remain unchanged. Both sessions are terminal; five private files are retained inactive and the original parent is absent. Observed Oracle CLEAR covers truthful failure classification and preservation only.

Integration artifact repairs are exhausted at 3 of 3. V1aj.4 and dependent V1ai.4 closure stop here; no guard edit, recompile, producer rerun or fourth recovery is authorized. The 34-fact / 95-method registration remains prepared; this fresh chain produced no fact classification or final-readiness outcome. [Blocked work](../BLOCKED.md) records the missing follow-up scope and next action. Full M0/roadmap, coverage targets/branch arms, optimized cache and all unanswered owner/data/public/provider/platform decisions remain open.

This documentation checkpoint updates only existing document identities in the profile. It is reviewed metadata, **not native-reverified**. Native receipts retain the tested draft 0.109 epoch; later documentation review does not qualify the current checkout. Exact hashes and review scope are retained in the ignored checkpoint receipt.

**Current independent checkpoint (draft 0.112):** required `LOCAL-RM-DISCOVERY` core trimming/NativeAOT analyzer discovery is verified in scope. [Probe contract 0.2](modernization-aot-feasibility-probe.md) and [ATLAS](modernization-aot-feasibility-hardness.md) bind unchanged28 core files, authentic reviewed graphs, cold locked restores, actual Csc/analyzer activation and45 unique source-bound errors (25 trimming / 20 AOT) versus ordinary0 warnings/errors. Observed Oracle CLEAR includes467 sealed input/snapshot pairs, inactive parent retirement and unchanged source/index/336 ordinary binaries; both genuine runner failures remain preserved. Remaining discovery and all owner/data/public/provider/platform/coverage/full-roadmap gates stay open.

## Requirements and completion inventory

The `LOCAL-RM-*` identifiers below are local trace labels for sections and milestone rows in [the roadmap](modernization-roadmap.md), not new product requirements. Implementation is complete only when all adopted required outcomes have direct evidence and every conditional row has an explicit selection/probe outcome. A decision to defer a candidate is recorded as deferred, never as an implemented provider. M0 alone is not the objective.

| Trace | Obligation / selection | Required outcome | Delivery and evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| LOCAL-RM-M0 | Required foundation | Correct query caches, array/null semantics, mapping/cache identity, resource ownership; repeatable build/test/package and compatibility checks; one release metadata source. | V1 and subsequent M0 ownership/package slices; regressions, consumer smoke, package inspection, declared CI. |
| LOCAL-RM-M1 | Required modernization | Actual async query/command/transaction I/O, cancellation, async streaming/disposal, scope isolation; optional DI/hosting, redacted logs, tracing and metrics; distinct failure outcomes. | Separate M1 end-to-end slices with live async-driver and disposal evidence; SQLite alone cannot prove genuine async I/O. |
| LOCAL-RM-MAPPING | M1q modernization; workload-selected bounded slices, explicit deferral for remaining features | Records/immutable constructors, nullable metadata, converters, modern date/time, JSON/arrays and missing/null; explicit tenant/soft-delete policies and bypass checks. | Mapping and policy slices with concrete acceptance cases and per-provider exclusions; every item is selected or explicitly deferred at the workload/contract gate. |
| LOCAL-RM-QUERY | M1q modernization; workload-selected provider capabilities, explicit deferral for remaining features | Deterministic keyset/cursor pagination, parameterized native escape hatches, query previews/capability failures, conditional writes, optimistic concurrency, partial-failure batches/bulk operations. | Query-family contracts and engine-specific acceptance evidence; no implicit scan or hidden client evaluation; every item has a selected slice or recorded deferral. |
| LOCAL-RM-M2 | Adopted first relational milestone; environment/contract gates remain | PostgreSQL/Npgsql relational provider: queries, CRUD, generated keys, transactions, pagination, parameters and selected types. | M1 plus SQL capability design gate; pinned real PostgreSQL integration and package-consumer checks. |
| LOCAL-RM-M3 | Adopted first managed NoSQL milestone; AWS/contract gates remain | DynamoDB typed keyed access, partition/sort queries, projections/filters, explicit indices/consistency, opaque continuation, capacity/budgets, conditional conflicts, opt-in bounded scans and credential chain. | Approved keyed contract, deterministic contract evidence plus real AWS-specific checks in a disposable authorized environment; preserve empty-page continuation. |
| LOCAL-RM-M4A | Adopted vector experiments; quality/workload gates remain | pgvector then Qdrant experiments before a stable retrieval contract; dimensions/types/metrics/model metadata, exact/approximate options, filters/isolation, score direction/rank, cancellation and quality evidence. | Real engines and approved relevance corpus; pgvector depends on PostgreSQL. Milvus receives a SDK/server compatibility probe before a provider commitment. |
| LOCAL-RM-M4B | Workload-selected search capability; selection gate before implementation | Separate OpenSearch text-search capability with mappings/analyzers, filters, facets/highlights, bounded pagination, indexing/refresh and explicit partial failures. | Real OpenSearch search corpus, visibility, isolation and failure checks; hybrid ranking adds its own pipeline/quality evidence if selected. |
| LOCAL-RM-M5 | Adopted DuckDB milestone; ClickHouse conditional on remote workload | DuckDB analytical reads, projections/aggregates, parameter binding, streaming, explicit file/import configuration and native architecture support; ClickHouse follows selection of a remote analytical workload. | SQL capability design gate, actual engines, write/transaction boundaries and runtime compatibility. |
| LOCAL-RM-M6 | Required demand/selection gate; implement the selected model only | Demand-led selection of a document, graph, keyed-cache or wide-column provider with a consuming application, maintainer and advantage over its SDK. | Owner selects model/workload; implement its distinct contract and certify named versions. CouchDB/MongoDB, Neo4j, Valkey and Cassandra are candidates, not automatic commitments to all four. |
| LOCAL-RM-DISCOVERY | Required named probes; remaining families deferred discovery | Verify MariaDB compatibility; probe Milvus client parity, vector abstraction interoperability and trimming/NativeAOT feasibility. Distributed SQL/time-series/geospatial/embedded documents remain demand-led discovery. | Bounded recorded probe outcomes and explicit support/exclusion decisions; no inferred protocol compatibility or blanket AOT promise. |
| LOCAL-RM-LATER | Conditional follow-ons; owner selection required | CTEs/window functions, richer aggregates, source generation, DynamoDB batch/transaction/TTL/change-stream slices, and search-index synchronization sample. | Preserve as follow-on scope requiring workload/contract decisions and expanded plans; do not silently delete or mark implemented. |
| LOCAL-RM-SUPPORT | Required for every implemented capability | Experimental/supported/legacy status, version/OS matrices, runnable examples, separate integration skips; additive 1.x APIs and preview packages, approved major breaks only. | Final documentation, actual engine/SDK evidence and owner promotion decision; Git tags alone do not prove NuGet availability. |

## Project fact ledger

These are project-scoped registers for this implementation. The34 historical rows below and their native bindings retain their exact claims and scope. The [complete future obligation ledger](modernization-future-facts.md) is also part of this plan: versioned Proposed records cover the full retained required/conditional roadmap now, while selection, contract, data and exact evidence expansion still gate activation. `Proposed` records are obligations, not completion claims. Do not reuse an evidence ID or change semantics without the recorded requirement-owner process.

| Fact | Kind / lifecycle | Claim and requirement | Evidence / oracle / result |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| BASE.SYNC.v1 | Characterization / Active | Baseline local CRUD, transaction rollback, translation and compiled-query cases remain green (LOCAL-RM-M0, LOCAL-RM-M1). | EV-BASE: existing MSTest and translation harness; observed baseline is 7 pass / 325 skipped plus 247 pass. Inherited fixtures are protected cases, not new representative evidence. |
| CACHE.EXECUTE.v1 | Behavior / Proposed | Given a supported IQueryable, untyped cache execution returns its executed result, not an equality boolean (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-EXEC: new focused public API regression; historical untyped RED repaired by V1c; broader characterization remains required. |
| CACHE.LOOKUP.v1 | Behavior / Proposed | Cache lookup returns the matching cached object; a miss with add:false returns no entry. A miss with add:true returns the inserted item through the out parameter while the Boolean records whether an existing entry was found. Add-enabled accesses update recency without cross-entry substitution (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-LOOKUP: ordered hits/misses/eviction/concurrent calls; equality/result oracle, generated-data gate V0.2. |
| CACHE.ISOLATION.v1 | Behavior / Proposed | A shared compiled-query cache cannot reuse another provider's mapping or policy for the same CLR query root (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-ISOLATION: two explicitly different SQLite provider policies/mappings; result/translation oracle, V0.2. |
| ARRAY.NATIVE.NONEMPTY.v1 | Characterization / Observed scoped; phase gate open | Ordinary real-provider queries retain current nonempty native array captures through compiler normalization/SQL binding, uncached and cached (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-ARRAY-NATIVE: actual acquisition/compiler signatures, independent native multisets and A→B→A on the same query, V1a; no null/boundary or explicit delegate claim. |
| ARRAY.NULL.v1 | Behavior / Proposed | Normalized array/span membership treats a null implicit span as empty; explicit Enumerable null behavior is preserved (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-ARRAY: null/empty/nonempty and changing capture regressions; current rewrite may already satisfy them, so verification may start green. |
| RESOURCE.OWNERSHIP.v1 | Behavior / Proposed | Commands/readers and provider-owned connection leases release on success, errors and early disposal; borrowed connections remain owned by the caller (LOCAL-RM-M0, LOCAL-RM-M1). | EV-RESOURCE: real SQLite and instrumented lifetime checks; remaining eager/batch ownership paths require separate semantic RED under reviewed contracts. |
| CACHE.NATIVE.EXECUTE.v1 | Behavior / Observed scoped; phase gate open | Untyped, typed and expression cache APIs execute unchanged native SQLite metadata sequences instead of yielding an equality Boolean (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-NATIVE-EXEC: direct captured sequence oracle and compilation control, V1c.1 → V1c.2. Broad generated/cache obligations stay open. |
| CACHE.NATIVE.LOOKUP.v1 | Behavior / Observed scoped; phase gate open | Configured sequential MRU lookups over unchanged live metadata return the matching cached entry, default on a nonadding miss, and preserve explicit promotion/eviction semantics (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-NATIVE-LOOKUP: native distinct strings and duplicate function names for canonical identity; V1c.1 → V1c.2. No generated schedules or boundary values. |
| CACHE.NATIVE.PROVIDER.v1 | Behavior / Observed scoped; phase gate open | Distinct live SQLite provider roots retain their actual metadata mapping and configured native filter when using a shared query cache (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-NATIVE-PROVIDER: installed schema acquisition, independent native SQL result multisets and actual execution logs; V1c.5 Verify after root correction. Same-root policy mutation and tenant security remain unproved. |
| CACHE.NATIVE.ROOT.v1 | Behavior / Observed scoped; phase gate open | A shared cache does not substitute the result of another distinct captured real-metadata IQueryable root with the same CLR type (LOCAL-RM-M0). | EV-CACHE-NATIVE-ROOT: distinct native version/options sources, V1c.3 → V1c.4 after lookup fixes; broader provider/mapping/policy isolation remains separate. |
| COMMAND.NORMAL.DISPOSAL.v1 | Behavior / Observed scoped implementation; phase gate open | Raw and executor nonquery paths dispose their created command before returning after successful execution; a caller-opened connection stays open (LOCAL-RM-M0). No worker may edit another packet's files or the status register. Ask the coordinator to revise ownership before shared-file edits. A stopped or failed worker must be confirmed terminal before its scope is reassigned. Single-agent fallback executes the same graph sequentially. ROOT owns plan revisions and integrates one green packet at a time; independent read-only review may overlap.

## GOTCHA spec: modernization execution

**Goals:** Implement the roadmap's modern data-access capabilities while preserving supported synchronous behavior.
**Objectives:** Concrete regression evidence, actual async driver operations, tested backend contracts, consumable packages and truthful provider support status.
**Tasks:** Start from inspected source; terminate only when the completion inventory has direct evidence or the affected work is explicitly blocked awaiting a required external decision/access.
**Capabilities:** Repository edits, .NET build/test/pack, Serena where operational, official external documentation, authorized bounded subagents. No paid resources, publication or production changes.
**Health:** No imposed token budget; three repair attempts per failure; root monitors handles and input changes, not inferred worker intent.
**Attributes:** Preserve existing files and public sync contracts; never fabricate representative data, weaken tests, imply unavailable certification or substitute Task.Run for async I/O.
**Constraints:** .NET 10/Linux ARM64 now; x64/Windows evidence must come from corresponding environments. Unsupported services remain explicit gates.
**Users:** Application developers and provider authors described in the roadmap.
**Runtime:** Persistent plan/status/evidence plus transient worker messages; investigate, establish RED, implement, verify, independently review, integrate. New evidence changes task readiness.
**Beliefs and intentions:** Scoped cache/resource fixes and verified evidence exist; broader reliability, coverage and decision gates remain incomplete. Repair and verify the remaining existing-system defects before expanding execution models and database families. V1aa specifically gates GetCommand initialization-failure ownership through native acquisition and a separate Test/Implement pair.

## Phase V0: baseline and execution prerequisites

**Role:** Foundation. **Depends on:** None. **Facts introduced:** None. **Facts strengthened/protected:** BASE.SYNC.v1.
**Execution baseline:** `60ddef6` and the untracked roadmap; commands recorded above. **Integration:** WP-PLAN, root only.
**Verification/demo:** Baseline commands and then `bash eng/verify.sh` once V0.4 creates it.
**Outcome:** A reproducible baseline and explicit decisions/data gates, not new provider support.
**Rollback:** Remove only newly created task-owned tooling/docs; preserve roadmap and original user files.
**Executed by:** Root and read-only investigation workers; model identifiers Unknown; current results recorded in AGENT_STATE.md.

**Provider boundary:** Existing SQLite/SQL translation. **Work packet/write scope:** WP-PLAN; status record only.

### Task V0.2: human decision — deterministic fixture authorization

**Type:** Human Decision. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0/LOCAL-RM-M1/LOCAL-RM-QUERY; DP-05.
**Decision needed:** Authorize deterministic in-code test fixtures for explicit boundary regressions and lifetime/driver simulation checks?
**Why real data cannot serve:** Ordering a cache, null-span membership, exception/disposal timing and active-operation cancellation require controlled boundary states. Generated tests do not establish provider compatibility, production query distributions or search quality; those still require real engines/captures/corpora.
**Scope requested:** V1.1b package-consumer boundary inputs, V1.3 cache correctness/isolation inputs, V1.5 array-membership boundary inputs; later resource/async/mapping/provider-contract tasks require named amendments before they consume this permission.
**Generator:** Handwritten deterministic C# MSTest cases with .NET SDK 10.0.401/runtime 10.0.12; fixed case values, no random corpus generation. New generated tests live under `IQToolkitTest/Generated/` and carry this authorization reference.
**Determinism:** Fixed case values; any later randomized generator needs an explicit seed/version and expanded authorization.
**Covered tasks:** V1.1b, V1.3, V1.5 only at this dispatch horizon. **Facts affected:** PACKAGE.CONSUMER.v1, CACHE.EXECUTE/LOOKUP/ISOLATION.v1, ARRAY.NULL.v1.
**Owner/approver:** Project owner acting as platform/data owner. **Default if unanswered:** Not authorized.
**Real data dependency:** None. **Provider boundary:** N/A. **Work packet/write scope:** WP-PLAN; root records answer here.
**Inputs/contracts:** User question issued during implementation. **Depends on:** V0.1.
**Validation/handoff:** Record the actual human answer and exact scope before dependent test creation.
**Acceptance:** An explicit decision is preserved; unavailable representative data is not replaced by generated input.
**Current status:** The original C# fixture question remains unanswered. The project owner separately authorized deterministic Python 3.12 controller-state fixtures for MDB0.1 on 2026-10-06; the active MDB0.1a/1b scope is narrowed below to refusal and no-write accounting only. That decision does not resolve or extend the C# scope.

**Historical authorization and current restriction:** On 2026-10-06, the owner authorized deterministic Python 3.12 in-memory/private-temporary controller-state fixtures for then-scoped MDB0.1 recovery/refusal tests. For the current MDB0.1a/1b correction, only isolated stale-path refusal and no-write reservation-accounting tests remain active. Same-attempt recovery, rebind, journal, and resume tests are withdrawn from current execution and retained as historical evidence only; do not invoke their old runner or treat recovery as expected behavior. The current runner is `artifacts/mariadb-package-discovery/verify_mdb0_stale_lockout.py`, handwritten with fixed values and no random or external provider/package/domain payload. It may exercise only test fixtures in memory/private temporary paths. Facts affected: none. This narrowed scope does not authorize application facts, production data, database/provider behavior, network dispatch, or fixtures for any other task. Future fixture purposes require a separate scope-bound decision.

### Task V0.5: establish coverage and compatibility tooling

**Type:** Probe. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0/LOCAL-RM-M1, technical enabler — measurable verification.
**Real data dependency:** Existing inherited suites for baseline characterization only. **Provider boundary:** Installed Microsoft.CodeCoverage18.10.1 and SDK10.0.401 ApiCompat.
**Work packet/write scope:** WP-PLAN; eng/coverage.runsettings and measurement/compatibility runners. **Inputs/contracts:** Existing TestSdk18.10.1 transitive collector; no new dependency. **Depends on:** V0.1. **Facts protected:** BASE.SYNC.v1.
**Description:** Use static managed instrumentation with restoration on ARM64; inspect actual source report and binary hashes. Validate current packages against identified local v1.1.1 package baselines, without downloading an assumed published release.
**Validation/handoff:** `dotnet test IQToolkitTest/IQToolkitTest.csproj -c Release --no-build --no-restore --settings eng/coverage.runsettings --collect "Code Coverage;Format=Cobertura" --results-directory artifacts/coverage --logger "trx;LogFileName=coverage.trx"`; compare before/after SHA-256. For each project, pack with `EnablePackageValidation=true` and its absolute `PackageValidationBaselinePath`; never set a download baseline version. Root owns the exclusive instrumentation/build lease.
**Acceptance:** Nonempty actual-source report, unchanged test outcomes, restored binaries, recorded coverage scope/baseline and branch limitations; actual ApiCompat output, no suppressions. Source feasibility alone is not success. The collector does not measure unloaded MySQL/Access or the separate translation executable.

**Probe results and reference decision:** The collector produced 2295/8319 covered lines and 1230/5324 covered branches for core/Data/SqlClient/SQLite; all instrumented DLL/PDB hashes restored. The initial API comparison exposed missing MySQL type-forward references. The isolated validation-only restore now pins System.Drawing.Common10.0.8 and Microsoft.Win32.SystemEvents10.0.8, matching the installed Windows.Extensions10.0.8 dependency, and adds the installed SDK's System.Formats.Nrbf assembly. These are metadata search references, not runtime provider dependencies. Corrected tooling received Oracle CLEAR: it restores/rebuilds current sources, derives exact current package paths from SDK outputs and uses native SDK hashing/assembly/XML tasks. Final warn-as-error run `artifacts/api-compatibility/runs/run.ofwFIW2v` against the reproduced release records six fresh validations, zero unresolved references, unchanged package/source/reference hashes and unchanged external MySQL dependencies. Project dependency versions must follow the current package version. This proves static compatibility only. [SDK reference resolution](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/core/project-sdk/msbuild-props#packagevalidationreferencepath), [Drawing package dependency metadata](https://www.nuget.org/packages/System.Drawing.Common/10.0.8).

### Task V0.5b: reproduce the release API baseline

## Phase V1r: normal nonquery command ownership

**Role:** Capability, narrowly bounded M0 reliability slice. **Outcome:** The library releases its actual native nonquery commands after normal completion while preserving caller-opened connection ownership. The broader RESOURCE.OWNERSHIP.v1 and RAW.TRANSACTION.v1 facts remain proposed and unproved.
**Prerequisites:** V0.3 resolved; V0.4/V0.5 engineering and measured coverage available; V0.9a/c reviewed; existing synchronous/API evidence available. V0.2 generated-input approval is not consumed: this packet uses live provider metadata only. V0.7 remains pending; no phase/final coverage exception or closure is inferred.

### Task V1r.1: observe native command disposal before teardown

**Type:** Test. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Fact/evidence:** COMMAND.NORMAL.DISPOSAL.v1, Tier 1 → EV-COMMAND-NORMAL.
**Real data dependency:** [SQLite running-library metadata](https://www.sqlite.org/lang_corefunc.html#sqlite_version), [native in-memory configuration](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/standard/data/sqlite/connection-strings), installed Microsoft.Data.Sqlite10.0.12. The [version-matched connection implementation](https://github.com/dotnet/efcore/blob/v10.0.12/src/Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.Core/SqliteConnection.cs) exposes virtual CreateCommand; the observer returns the exact base-created native command unchanged. [Component.Disposed](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/api/system.componentmodel.component.disposed?view=net-10.0) provides the native disposal event. No generated records, payloads, expected version strings, mutations, errors or simulator are involved.
**Work packet/write scope:** WP-COMMAND-NORMAL, root only; IQToolkitTest/RealProvider/CommandOwnershipTests.cs. No shared project/package changes.
**Depends on:** Prerequisites above; Oracle has independently reviewed real-source feasibility, not granted fixture permission.
**Expected RED signature:** Native command disposal events are absent after successful raw/executor execution on a caller-opened connection, asserted before observer/connection teardown. Closed-start cases may pass from SQLite's incidental connection-close cleanup; they cannot alone establish the fact. Setup/import failures are not valid RED.
**Description:** Add one native-event control using explicit consumer disposal, plus raw and executor command cases with actual initially open/closed connections. Execute SELECT sqlite_version() without fabricated arguments; inspect actual command identities/events and connection state. The observer only registers native events, and never disposes on the library's behalf. Scope excludes reader, batch, failure, cancellation, parameter and transaction claims.
**Validation/handoff:** Run filtered Release MSTest with strict flags and save RED log/TRX plus source identity under artifacts/command-ownership/. Confirm the native-event control passes and both borrowed-connection cases fail for missing disposal before proceeding.
**Acceptance:** Expected semantic RED observed; existing data is untouched; provenance and resource assertions precede test teardown.

### Task V1r.2: dispose normal nonquery commands

**Type:** Implement. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Makes green:** EV-COMMAND-NORMAL. **Depends on:** V1r.1 with observed semantic RED.
**Real data dependency:** Same live engine metadata as V1r.1; no generated data authorization.
**Work packet/write scope:** WP-COMMAND-NORMAL, root only; the raw ExecuteCommand(string) and Executor.ExecuteCommand(QueryCommand, object[]) command declarations in IQToolkit.Data/DbEntityProvider.cs. Root also owns integrated plan/state/evidence reports.

Dispose before releasing the lease and preserve return values, rowsAffected, public signatures and transaction/parameter setup. Do not modify query readers, batches, eager/deferred enumeration, raw transaction semantics or other provider overrides.
**Validation/handoff:** Filtered GREEN, ordinary strict gate, six fresh API comparisons and package inspection; collect scoped coverage with restoration manifests if available. Oracle reviews actual RED/GREEN, source delta and evidence scope. Record measured limitations rather than assuming coverage approval or full ownership certification.
**Acceptance:** All five focused observations pass; baseline suites remain green; warnings/errors stay zero; API/package evidence passes. Keep wider ownership/final-phase obligations and unresolved coverage targets open.
**Observed checkpoint:** Retained identical test source: RED 2 fail/3 pass; GREEN 5 pass. Strict run.niFdPZiA: zero warnings/errors, 12 passes/325 skips, 247 translations. API run.5UTsrF9A: six comparisons and package inspection pass. Collector coverage.jkzq2j8t: 2300/8300 lines, 1232/5314 branches; all 336 captured binary hashes restored immediately. Raw method retains an uncovered legacy logging branch (50% method branches); executor method is fully covered in this instrumented report. No changed-file/final target or comprehensive cleanup qualification is claimed. Separate [ATLAS report](modernization-command-ownership-hardness.md) records limitations; phase closure remains open.
**GOTCHA scope:** Goal: stop normal-path command leaks. Objectives: real native events after both nonquery calls while preserving actual connection state. Trigger: inspected undisposed commands; termination: semantic RED then verified GREEN and independent review. Capabilities: local code/test edits, native SQLite and serial .NET gates; off-limits: fabricated data, injected failure, driver-default changes, broad ownership fixes or remote actions. Constraints: only Linux ARM64 and success-path evidence; wider coverage/fixtures remain pending. Users: synchronous toolkit consumers. Runtime: root owns shared files and build lease; record source identities and results. Belief: caller-preopened paths leave commands undisposed; intention: prove and repair precisely those paths.


## Phase V1c: cache regressions with live native metadata

**Role:** Capability, required M0 cache reliability. **Outcome:** Execute cache queries and sequential lookups correctly on real native metadata; independently repair distinct-root substitution. This is additional actual-source evidence, not approval or replacement of the V0.2 generated-input request or broad V1.3 scope.
**Prerequisites:** Selected standard V0.3; usable strict/API/coverage tooling; V1r integrated; actual local SQLite. Oracle feasibility review accepts unchanged live rows, positive capacity as configuration and declared sequential actions as real-source evidence. Missing source rows are a data prerequisite failure, not a reason to invent values. Coverage targets and V0.2 remain unresolved; no phase closure is inferred.

### Task V1c.1: establish public cache execution and lookup RED

**Type:** Test. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Fact/evidence:** CACHE.NATIVE.EXECUTE/LOOKUP.v1, Tier 1 → EV-CACHE-NATIVE-EXEC/LOOKUP.
**Real data dependency:** Installed SQLite native [compile options](https://www.sqlite.org/pragma.html#pragma_compile_options) and [function metadata](https://www.sqlite.org/pragma.html#pragma_function_list); native version/source metadata, existing driver locks and unchanged returned strings. No invented option/function names, transformed payloads, generated values or schedules. Positive capacity is cache configuration; fixed sequential actions are the required lookup contract, not a synthetic dataset.
**Work packet/write scope:** WP-CACHE-NATIVE, root: IQToolkitTest/RealProvider/CacheMetadataTests.cs and SQLiteMetadata.cs; root owns plan/state/ATLAS. No shared package/project changes.
**Depends on:** Prerequisites above. **Protects:** BASE.SYNC.v1 and COMMAND.NORMAL.DISPOSAL.v1.
**Expected RED:** Untyped API yields a Boolean instead of the real captured sequence; MRU head hit returns another captured option, a miss returns an unrelated cached value, and add-enabled hits fail to promote before eviction. Setup/import/data-prerequisite failures do not count. The native typed/expression compilation control must run successfully before treating these as semantic cache failures.
**Description:** Capture actual native responses and preserve the exact observed values. Test typed/expression controls, untyped execution, hits/misses, add semantics, capacity eviction, nonadding-hit recency, Clear and canonical cached identity from native duplicate function names. Require source cardinality/duplicates and fail without fabricated fallbacks. Save native provenance/observations to TRX.
**Validation/handoff:** Filtered strict Release MSTest, unique per-attempt artifacts/command-cache/runs; save source copies/hashes, logs and TRX before V1c.2. Prove failures bind to the required semantics; first-green protecting controls are legitimate.
**Acceptance:** Actual semantic RED is retained with a passing native compilation control; no generated data or silent authorization.

### Task V1c.2: repair executed overload and sequential MRU behavior

**Type:** Implement. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Makes green:** EV-CACHE-NATIVE-EXEC/LOOKUP. **Depends on:** V1c.1 semantic RED.
**Real data dependency:** Same unchanged live metadata as V1c.1.
**Work packet/write scope:** WP-CACHE-NATIVE, root: QueryCache.Execute(IQueryable) and MostRecentlyUsedCache.Lookup. Public signatures and locking/version machinery remain protected; no unrelated refactor or constructor/boundary policy change.
**Description:** Dispatch the untyped overload to expression execution. ### Task V1c.6: decide cached policy replacement semantics

**Type:** Human Decision. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Depends on:** Actual source inspection of EntityProvider.Policy, EntityPolicy.Apply, QueryCache and QueryCompiler. **State:** Asked asynchronously; unanswered. No preselected choice is authorization.

**Decision needed:** Should assigning a replacement Policy automatically invalidate automatic QueryCache entries, require the caller to Clear the cache, or freeze policy after compilation? Explicit QueryCompiler.Compile delegates retain the policy under which they were compiled; clearing an automatic cache cannot replace delegates already held by callers. The question presents automatic replacement invalidation as the recommendation, not as an adopted contract.

**Scope and consequences:** An identity-only replacement rule cannot detect in-place EntityPolicy.Apply or arbitrary custom QueryPolicy mutation. Clearing a shared cache also invalidates unrelated provider entries. Record the owner's choice and those limits before drafting a dependent semantic Test→Implement packet. Native characterization may observe actual cached/fresh behavior while this is pending, but must not enshrine stale behavior as the desired contract. No policy implementation, snapshot/freeze API or tenant-security claim is authorized by this unanswered task.

API run.WzuGcqaM: six comparisons, all 176 compiler inputs matched at this historical V1c checkpoint; six library/symbol pairs pass inspection. Collector coverage.lbnr2aig: 2540/8305 lines (30.58399%), 1384/5312 branches (26.05422%), all 336 captured DLL/PDB hashes restored immediately. Pair, provider and final integrated Oracle reviews are CLEAR. The [cache ATLAS report](modernization-cache-hardness.md) records exact run identities and uncovered algorithm/coverage/context obligations. Broad V1.3 and full phase closure remain open.

**Type:** Verify; legitimate first-green protection. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0/SUPPORT. **Fact/evidence:** PACKAGE.INDEPENDENT.v1, Tier 1 → EV-PACKAGE-INDEPENDENT.
**Real data dependency:** Actual nupkg ID/version/dependencies, package hashes, installed SDK and real NuGet resolution/source metadata; existing public symbols. No provider payloads, user tables, entity records or generated fixtures.

Compile named public package symbols and relevant declared transitive toolkit/driver symbols. Access references stay inside an explicit Windows-only compile boundary without analyzer suppression; no backend execution or platform support claim. Each preparation/verification uses a fresh outside-repository project and cold cache, explicit source mapping (toolkit local-only), pre-copy snapshots/copy checks and final original stability. NuGet's logical content hash excludes signature metadata; `.nupkg.sha512` describes complete archive bytes. Preserve that real failure and inspect its actual metadata before correcting mechanics: always compare raw archive hash with its sidecar, compare logical content hash across lock/assets/source metadata, and require an actual signature entry whenever those domains differ. Unsigned toolkit packages additionally bind both hashes to the exact tested feed. Unchanged locked SDK restore enforces external logical content and dependency edges; the framework-only verifier does not reimplement NuGet signing. No resolved data, expected hashes or fixtures are replaced. Current joint strict run.5GPZMLxq50pass/325skip/247translations, API run.A9nS44yE six comparisons/packages, SQLite run.Ix9C3QR6 and six independent run.tWm4quSN consumers pass. ### Task V1b.1: reproduce streamed batch command leaks

**Type:** Test. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Fact/evidence:** COMMAND.BATCH.DISPOSAL.v1, component/native Tier1 → EV-COMMAND-BATCH. **Depends on:** Prerequisites above. **Write scope:** WP-BATCH-COMMAND new test file only, private helpers/observers inside it; root plan/evidence.

Acquire actual sqlite_version/source and PRAGMA compile_options schema/results using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite10.0.12. Allocate each transport buffer using actual reader.FieldCount, call GetValues and require every field copied unchanged. Require exactly one string column and at least two actual responses; keep the first two without synthesizing values. On a separate ordinary native connection, bind these same response fields to `SELECT compile_options FROM pragma_compile_options WHERE compile_options = @name`; acquire actual ExecuteNonQuery counts and exactly one actual reader string per option. Missing/ambiguous responses fail acquisition. Create QueryParameter with the captured CLR type and the provider's actual TypeSystem.GetColumnType; batchSize50 is the existing public default, not a generated boundary case.

Use transparent connection/reader observers returning base native resources unchanged. Seven cases: an explicit native disposal-event control; nonquery/generic borrowed-open streamed full/early cases; and nonquery/generic initially-closed buffering/release controls. Borrowed streams acquire no command/reader before MoveNext; full results equal native oracles, or the first early result equals its oracle. Dispose active enumerators before teardown; command events must fire, actual generic readers must close, and borrowed connections remain open. In the early generic case, the actual reader is open at the yield before disposal. Initially-closed stream:true executions already buffer all native operations before returning; inspect completed command/reader disposal and closed connection before consuming their returned lists. Those cases do not characterize early native enumeration.

**Expected RED:** Four borrowed-open cases fail specifically for absent command disposal. Native control and closed-start controls remain green because connection closure may mask the missing declaration. Acquisition/import/setup failures are not semantic RED. ## Phase V1f: native eager reader-acquisition failure ownership

**Role:** Capability, remaining M0 resource ownership. **Target capability slice:** Release an acquired command when the actual native ExecuteReader rejects the operation before returning a reader. **Phase dependencies/baseline:** Integrated V1b/V1e CLEAR; branch and selected standard unchanged. V0.2/V0.7/V1c.6 remain unanswered and are not consumed. Root owns source and exclusive SDK lease; Oracle reviews read-only.

**Facts introduced:** COMMAND.NATIVE.ACQUISITION.FAILURE.v1, Tier1→EV-COMMAND-NATIVE-FAILURE. **Facts protected:** BASE.SYNC, prior command/cache/mapping/public/package contracts. **Verification command:** `GITHUB_ACTIONS=true bash eng/verify.sh`, final API/package/exact-feed consumers and current coverage/restoration. **Demo/validation command:** Focused native test commands below. **Observable outcome:** An actual native exception propagates unchanged, no reader returns to the toolkit observer, its acquired command disposes before teardown, and caller-owned connection remains open. **Rollback notes:** Revert only V1f acquisition-boundary source change if protecting evidence regresses; preserve native captures and preceding ownership fixes. **Exit criteria:** Actual acquisition and valid semantic RED→unchanged GREEN, scoped integrated checks/ATLAS/Oracle; broader phase closure and mandatory coverage targets remain open.

**Data/authority routing:** Oracle confirmed that a live engine rejection for fixed `DELETE FROM pragma_compile_options;` is an actual provider response rather than a generated exception or fixture. Authored SQL is operation configuration, analogous to accepted metadata SELECTs. [SQLite PRAGMA functions](https://www.sqlite.org/pragma.html#pragma_functions) describe the metadata source; [virtual-table update rules](https://www.sqlite.org/vtab.html#the_xupdate_method) describe read-only modules. Version-matched [SQLite3.53.3 pragma.c](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sqlite/sqlite/version-3.53.3/src/pragma.c) defines xUpdate=0; its [release manifest](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sqlite/sqlite/version-3.53.3/manifest.uuid) matches the native source hash d4c0e51e...782c62. The actual [driver acquisition](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dotnet/efcore/v10.0.12/src/Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.Core/SqliteCommand.cs) can allocate internally before returning; no returned-reader claim means no reader returned to the toolkit. This is a prospective routing decision, not acquisition evidence or a fixture waiver. Acquire exact installed failure before dependent toolkit assertions; never hardcode SQLITE_READONLY or substitute a fabricated error.

### Task V1j.1: Probe cold QueryCache and MRU invocation overlap

**Type:** Probe. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Question:** Actual overlapping cold calls preserve selection isolation and canonical insertion in this bounded workload? **Hypothesis:** Existing per-call arguments/locked MRU publication preserve these observed results; duplicate compilation is allowed. **Expected failure signature:** N/A; no artificial RED. **Fact/evidence:** Two independently qualified characterization bindings, never a combined all-concurrency fact. **Depends on:** V1j.0 acquired/reviewed inputs. **Scope/packet:** Same temporary harness/private observers, WP-NATIVE-CACHE-PROBE; max production code zero. **Real data/boundary:** Unchanged eligible native strings/read-only source; public QueryCache.Execute and MRU.Lookup, actual Task.Run scheduling only.

For QueryCache, construct one read-only actual native sequence and a single AsQueryable root. One shared query-building helper captures a different actual option for each filter, with identical expression shape and unchanged closures. For each fresh QueryCache(2), submit two ordinary Task.Run actors, each making exactly one Execute call. Record Stopwatch.GetTimestamp immediately before/after Execute returns or throws and actual managed thread identity. Enumerate each actor's returned sequence afterward, outside that invocation window. Compare each result against direct filtering/enumeration of the original capture outside IQToolkit; after both finish require Count1 and both expressions present. No prewarming, root replacement or shared connection.

For MRU, two actors each make one Lookup(actualName,true,out cached) on a fresh MostRecentlyUsedCache<string>(2), with the acquired equal/different-reference names. Record call windows/thread identities, actual Boolean returns and cached references. After both finish require Count1 and quiescent nonadding lookup; both returned references equal the retained canonical object, which must be one of the two actual acquired references. Do not assert a specific winner, ordering, Boolean-return history or one-compilation guarantee.

**Decision rule, fixed before execution:** Enforce all functional assertions on every attempt regardless of overlap; retain every attempt/actual exception. Each binding requires distinct actual threads and strictly overlapping invocation intervals. Bound each case independently to at most three fresh-cache attempts/two calls each, stop once eligible overlap is observed. No overlap throughout fails that focused Probe explicitly as `OverlapPrerequisiteUnmet`; it is not a cache defect, semantic RED, passing skip or concurrency GREEN. Actual functional failure stops the case, records a defect, and requires a separately registered Test→Implement before any library repair. Invocation overlap cannot establish overlap inside a critical section, version-rescan branch execution, linearizability or general concurrency safety.

**Evidence:** Named case/attempt source/hashes/TRX, unchanged inputs, timestamps/frequency/thread IDs, results/errors/canonical-reference identities, independent expected results and qualification reason. **Off-limits:** Barriers, sleeps, comparer pauses/hooks, forced interleavings, random schedules, repetitive workload padding, manufactured fixture/error/state, prewarming or extra dependencies. **Literal validation:** `dotnet test IQToolkitTest/IQToolkitTest.csproj -c Release --no-restore -p:ContinuousIntegrationBuild=true -warnaserror --filter FullyQualifiedName~NativeCacheConcurrencyProbe --logger 'trx;LogFileName=tests.trx' --results-directory <unique-cache-probe-directory>`. Three discovered methods (acquisition plus two independently assessed Probes); no unexpected skips/empty discovery. Framework pass counts alone never establish the overlap fact.

**Type:** Probe. **PRD trace:** LOCAL-RM-M0. **Question/hypothesis:** Both targets discard the GetOrAdd return value after a cold miss; actual overlapping initializers may return a locally created losing object while quiescent lookup returns the retained winner. This source mechanism is a hypothesis, not an observed defect. **Expected failure signature:** N/A before experiment; actual canonical mismatch classified separately. **Depends on:** V1k.0 live acquired/reviewed inputs. **Scope:** Same temporary harness/private observation helpers; maximum production code zero. **Data/boundaries:** Actual existing immutable native mapping configuration, AttributeMapping.GetEntity and EntityProvider.GetTable(MappingEntity); real Task.Run/runtime scheduling only.

For mapping, a fresh AttributeMapping(typeof(CompileOptionsContext)) receives two ordinary actor calls to GetEntity(typeof(NativeMetadataEntry),nameof(CompileOptionsContext.Entries)). For tables, use a fresh actual SQLite provider/connection and AttributeMapping per attempt; acquire exactly one mapping entity before launch, leaving target table cache cold. Two ordinary actors call provider.GetTable(theSameEntity), not the generic overload that would mix mapping initialization. Stop that target and register/review a separate requirement-owned Test→Implement before repair. Functional exceptions/incorrect native rows are retained actual failures, not hidden as timing results. Distinct-thread strictly overlapping invocation windows with same canonical returns support only that recorded successful acquisition observation; stop that target. Invocation overlap alone does not prove both creation paths executed. Assess targets separately; never replace a mismatch with a later passing attempt.

**Role:** Support for LOCAL-RM-M0 and DOTNET-GLOB-001's existing machine-protocol requirement, already recorded in [standard adoption](standards/adoption.md). **Contract:** SQL-DOUBLE-CULTURE-1. **Depends on:** Scoped V1t integrated CLEAR and actual source/data/prospective review. **Ownership:** Root source/SDK; agents read-only. **Planning horizon:** V1u.0 acquisition is observed and independently reviewed CLEAR. Historical pre-repair investigation: [SqlFormatter.WriteValue](../IQToolkit.Data/Common/Language/SqlFormatter.cs) grouped Single/Double, used ambient `value.ToString()` and appended `.0` when no period existed. Public `SqlFormatter.Format(Expression)` and inherited `SQLiteFormatter.Format(Expression)` expose this branch. Actual retained semantic RED now confirms the culture-dependent column-shape defect; current source uses an explicit invariant provider, with the original suffix mechanics preserved, and the unchanged cases pass GREEN. SQLite's [numeric literal contract](https://www.sqlite.org/lang_expr.html#literal_values_constants) uses a period regardless of locale. The selected standard requires explicit culture-independent machine formats; intentionally culture-sensitive LINQ/user-data conversions are excluded.

**Type:** Probe (conditional; non-dispatchable until full concrete static expansion). **PRD Trace:** LOCAL-RM-DISCOVERY. **Fact / Evidence:** EV-MARIADB-PACKAGE-STATIC, support only. **Expected Failure Signature / Makes Green:** N/A; empirical static observations. **Real Data Dependency:** MDB0.4 reviewed authentic raw archives/outer framing, extraction-readiness handoff, actual installed parser/readelf and host-library identities. **Provider Boundary:** Inert archive/control/license/script/ELF metadata only; no package execution or application DB boundary. **Work Packet / Write Scope:** WP-MDB0; root owns separately admitted static controller/profile/packet/manifests and private regular-file copies; reviewers read-only. **Inputs / Contracts:** Raw observed acceptance and newly expanded static source/commands/format/byte/member/log/time/process/retirement contract; proposed readiness subset is reference material only. **Depends On:** MDB0.4 independent observed archive CLEAR plus independent static source/planning expansion and exact static packet CLEAR. **Facts Protected:** Entire MDB0 protected set.

**Question:** Which compression/tar/link/control/license/ELF formats actually occur in the ten pinned packages, can reviewed bounded static tools inspect them safely, and what concrete prerequisites remain for a private native experiment? **Hypothesis:** Installed system tools may support a subset of the real packages; feasibility remains unknown. **Experiment:** First reconcile actual raw framing with installed decoder/parser capabilities; expand and independently review the smallest needed static subset, then inspect authentic control/payload metadata and bounded private regular-file copies. **Evidence to collect:** Authentic framing and member inventories, exact Package/Version/Architecture, expanded byte/member/path/link/mode counts, license/script text hashes and line references, ARM64 ELF headers/DT_NEEDED/interpreter/SONAME/symbol-version/search-path findings, installed provider identities, counters, exits and unresolved links/libraries. **Decision rule:** Unsupported formats, quota excess, malformed framing, path/link ambiguity, missing library/version/interpreter or preservation/containment failure stop the affected static packet and preserve failure; fully observed successful static matching permits only preparation of a separate native packet, never runtime or support promotion. **Max production code allowed:** None. **Disposition:** Retain immutable output and original failures; retire only manifest-owned inactive temporary copies after review/settlement; experimental control bytes remain inactive evidence, never production dependency. **Affected slices:** Later MariaDB native/catalog/typed-driver/provider/writable SQL qualification; no application phase becomes ready solely from static success.

**Description:** Before dispatch, root must freeze actual archive/tool input hashes, supported formats and conservative allocation with full task/command expansion; record unavailable/unsupported format as a discovery gate, not a fabricated fixture or implicit decoder install. Reference ceilings from readiness are 160 MiB aggregate expanded tar/payload budget, 64 MiB individual regular file, 12,000 logical members, depth 32 and 16 MiB aggregate command output; they are proposed and not accepted authority until real controls and concrete admission enforce them.

Initial proposed time/resource contract is one parser at a time, 30 seconds wall / 20 CPU seconds per package, 240-second supervised stage, bounded address space/output and at most 512 installed readelf invocations with five-second leaves. Root must reconcile aggregate limits, command count, actual framing and remaining storage/attempt ledgers into the static packet rather than treating per-file ceilings as additive permission. Handoff includes actual static manifests/findings and independent observed review.

**Acceptance Criteria:** Static source/packet/readback gates bind the exact raw epoch; expanded/member/log/memory/process/time/storage caps enforced before allocation and each write. No tar extract-all or link materialization/dereference; reject dangerous/unsupported paths, duplicates, sparse/special entries and ambiguous layout; only private non-executable regular copies, original metadata recorded. Installed readelf may inspect regular ELF bytes under a literal hashed argv; never `ldd`, loader `--list`, `--help`/`--version` of downloads, maintainer/bootstrap scripts, dlopen/ctypes, server/client/provider, SDK, apt/dpkg install or service/Docker changes. Scripts/licenses are bounded inert text only. Match observed metadata/ARM64 architecture and static transitive library/version requirements or classify missing/ambiguous conditions; static matching cannot certify startup/ABI/kernel/authentication/SQL compatibility. All processes absent and manifest-owned cleanup/preservation counters verify before disposition; untested failure paths stay unverified.

## Verification and completion contract

Root owns shared verification. The phase gate is `bash eng/verify.sh` after V0.4; the extended gate is `bash eng/verify-final.sh` after V0.6; V0.5 coverage/tool evidence is required before behavioral dispatch; package phases additionally require their exact pack/consumer checks, and future providers require their own actual-engine gates. No new coverage tooling is installed until its dependency and compatible scope are recorded; measure changed behavior with meaningful boundary tests rather than copying implementation. Do not weaken existing assertions or turn failures into skips.

At phase closure, rerun the declared gates against the integrated snapshot, review the fact delta and implementation risks, and record executing workers, content identities, commands/results, data authorization and next action in AGENT_STATE.md. Oracle review supplements executable evidence. An ATLAS hardness report must record the actual edge cases, failure handling, concurrency, security and observed commands before any new feature or architecture slice is called done.

Full objective closure requires direct evidence for the entire completion inventory, actual backend certification where promised, explicit outcomes for conditional decisions/probes, documentation of every new public/configuration surface, and no unresolved required gates. Do not mark the goal complete because M0 passes, an SDK compiles or an emulator responds. Leave the goal active while independent ready work exists; report specific required input/access only when it actually prevents dependent work.
